Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The bio variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 287 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 60 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the bio decomposition. By minimality, the element a^287 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 60-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

For the inductive step, we decompose the bio structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 449. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
